## CURRENT OWNER ADDENDUM — PERMANENT APPOINTMENTS, OWNER SUPERSESSION, AND MATRIX-WIDE DRAIN — 2026-10-10

## FORX QUEUE RULE

The section above is the **current operational queue view**. Older queue summaries below remain historical provenance and do not override this current FORX readback.

A queue item leaves the active view only when:
- explicitly dispositioned;
- validly moved to scoped HOLD with owner + dependency + return condition; or
- verified closed by FORX-V.

### Dependency-aware ordering

FORX-W assigns stable packet IDs and records `BLOCKED_BY` / `UNLOCKS` relationships for material fixes.

If Fix B depends on Fix A, **Fix A inherits at least Fix B's effective priority** until A is verified complete. Dependent fixes remain WAITING rather than being allowed to deploy out of order.

Independent fixes may proceed in parallel.

Weighted continuation is a prioritization aid only; authority, safety, legal/rights, single-writer constraints, explicit Owner priority and other governing hard gates override numeric weighting.

### Dependency failure fallback

If the preferred fix cannot deploy because a dependency cannot be met, FORX must attempt an outcome-equivalent alternate before human gating:

1. **Option A** — primary fix;
2. **Option B** — materially equivalent alternate route;
3. **Option C** — materially equivalent longer / less efficient workaround;
4. **MASTER escalation** — if only a materially degraded result remains;
5. **Human gate** — only for an irreducible human-only dependency or after lawful autonomous routes are exhausted.

A dependency failure is treated as a routing/solution problem first. Existing AMX metrics govern materiality and visibility; FORX does not create a replacement KPI layer.

## OCC-01 — Active Dependency Resurfacing

**Current status:** PENDING GOVERNANCE RECONCILIATION / HOLD ON DISPOSITION CLAIM  
**Governance-ready:** YES for a narrow disposition: ACCEPT / MODIFY / REJECT / ALREADY-SUBSUMED.  
**Exact text recovered:** YES — GitHub issue #5.

**Source:**  
https://github.com/amillimatrix-eng/CARBON-Creation-Hub/issues/5

**Controlling source rule:**

> When any AMilliMATRiX worker identifies a materially useful connector, plugin, credential, upload, approval, account action, or other Owner-action dependency:
>
> 1. Persist it with explicit state: ACTIVE / SATISFIED / SUPERSEDED / NO-LONGER-REQUIRED.
> 2. If ACTIVE and still material, re-surface it at the next point where it blocks or materially limits quality.
> 3. Re-surface on relevance, not every turn; avoid nagging.
> 4. A correction, topic shift, worker handoff, chat interruption, or continuation must not silently clear it.
> 5. When the Owner satisfies the dependency, detect the newly available capability, mark SATISFIED, and resume the higher-capability route without requiring the Owner to explain again why it was needed.
> 6. Do not label a lower-capability result FINAL when a previously surfaced, readily satisfiable dependency still materially limits that result unless it was explicitly waived.
> 7. Dependency state must survive continuity recovery and be visible to the next authorized worker.

Companion source invariant:

> **CORRECTION ≠ STOP.**

**Reconciliation note:** the later Notion disposition for Mandate Liveness explicitly references “active dependency resurfacing / BlueTech intake” as related merge material and states that exact dependencies remain active/resurface when material. No standalone durable disposition for issue #5 itself was found. Therefore this index does **not** infer acceptance or closure for OCC-01.

# II. ADAPTIVE CAPABILITY & CAPACITY SCALING

**Family status:** SEPARATE BY OWNER DIRECTIVE.  
**Current status:** DISPOSITIONED — ACCEPTED / MERGED in durable Notion governance provenance.  
**Governance-ready:** NO new decision required unless governance reopens it.  
**Exact accepted rule recovered:** YES. No reconstruction from memory was used.

**Durable source:**  
https://app.notion.com/p/3ee8f8b56cd781b58737d04ffa4ccfb6?pvs=204

**Exact core rule:**

> **Do not grant maximum operational capacity before capability is evidenced. Increase capacity as competence is proven. Maintain or increase it while performance remains strong. Reduce capacity when performance, evidence quality, reliability, safety, or outcome truth regresses. Re-expand only after the affected capability is re-proven.**

## 2026-10-09 — INTAKE STATUS INTERPRETATION CONTRACT
- **Purpose:** prevent historical `PENDING`, `DEGRADED`, `OPEN`, `FAIL`, `HOLD`, or pre-repair labels from being misread as current state after a later disposition.
- **Precedence rule:** a page's explicit top-level `CURRENT DISPOSITION — CONTROLLING STATE` block governs current Intake interpretation unless a newer explicit controlling disposition exists.
- **Search rule:** a search hit containing the word `PENDING` is **not** evidence that the item is currently pending. Search is discovery only. Fetch the source and reconcile the controlling disposition before classifying state.
- **Historical rule:** labels explicitly marked `Historical status (superseded)`, `Historical lifecycle (superseded)`, or equivalent remain provenance only and must not drive current routing.
- **Split-state rule:** Intake state and execution state are separate. `INTAKE CLOSED / ASSIGNED` may coexist with `EXECUTION OPEN`; `PARTIAL MERGE / EXPLICIT HOLD` means only the named hold scope remains non-executable.
- **HOLD rule:** current HOLD is valid only when exact held scope, owner, blocker/reason, and return condition are stated.
- **Supersession rule:** old failure evidence remains true as history even after repair; current state comes from the latest explicit disposition/readback, not from the most alarming historical sentence.
- **Consumer requirement:** ROOT, MASTER/Librarian, FORX, iSCOPE, PRI, Banker, Reaper, builders and any continuity consumer must apply this status-precedence contract before acting on Intake-derived state.
- **Anti-drift invariant:** `SEARCH MATCH != CURRENT STATUS`. `HISTORICAL STATUS != LIVE STATE`. `INTAKE CLOSED != EXECUTION COMPLETE`.

## 2026-10-09 — BLUE STATE ADDENDUM — Blocked Attempt Recovery
- **Disposition:** **OWNER DISTINCTION ACCEPTED / MERGED / ACTIVE.**
- **Parent Blue State:** `AMX-BLUE-STATE-MANDATE-BINDING-V1`
- **Core invariant:** `ATTEMPTED + BLOCKED != TERMINAL STATE`.
- A blocked material action creates an **OPEN EXECUTION OBLIGATION**. The system must preserve what was being attempted, the furthest evidenced state, attempt receipt, exact blocker/scope, dedupe/idempotency condition, next lawful recovery action, current/recovery owner and original acceptance condition.
- **Same-owner path:** retry or reroute through an already-authorized equivalent path and continue to completion.
- **Cross-owner/authority path:** route a complete recovery parcel through ROOT/Intake for assignment to the existing responsible owner/repair/authority path.
- **Continuation:** receiving owner resumes from furthest evidenced state; do not restart from zero or duplicate external action.
- **Invalid state:** `BLOCKED_WITHOUT_RECOVERY_OWNER_OR_NEXT_ACTION`.
- **Closure:** only original acceptance readback or explicit governed cancellation/supersession closes the obligation.
- **Law:** `BLOCKED RECEIPT -> OPEN OBLIGATION -> RECOVERY OWNER -> COMPLETION / GOVERNED CANCELLATION`.
- **Anti-drift:** merely saying/reporting `BLOCKED` is not completion and is not a valid handoff.

## DEEP RESEARCH QUALIFICATION — IDLE OVERDRIVE STATE IS NOT PROOF OF WORKFLOW OUTAGE — 2026-10-12

Further code readback of `overdrive/runner.py` (blob `d71c75550c72daa80cc585c8fc93ee0a4ee0fcb9`) refines the interpretation of the unchanged state files:

- `tick()` re-derives `signals` and `claims` every invocation. When there are no PENDING adapter entries **and** the semantic projection of old/new signals and claims is equal, it restores the pre-scan raw signals/claims and returns WATCHING with pending=0 and state_changed=false without changing `state.json`.
- `VOLATILE_SCAN_KEYS` excludes `generated_at` and `last_seen_at` from semantic comparison. An unchanged `claims.json`/ `signals.json` blob can therefore be intentional even after a scan. The workflow file `.github/workflows/overdrive-recovery.yml` (blob `9bfdb4940d4150e2f39246b26584da75ff81a12e`) declares a five-minute GitHub Actions schedule and says workflow logs remain the heartbeat when tracked state is unchanged.
- Consequently, the last persisted `state.json.last_tick` of `2026-10-11T19:42:44.598828Z` and unchanged claims timestamp are **not sufficient evidence that the five-minute workflow is down**. Current GitHub Actions run logs could not be inspected via the connected tools in this pass.
- Independently verified truth remains: all 20 retained adapter queue entries are COMPLETED with receipt paths + SHA-256; pending=0; failed=0. The claims file snapshot is 84 total, 78 WAITING, 6 CLOSED, 0 READY/external-ready, and 10 WAITING claims have original due_at values in the past.
- The separate Librarian scheduled automation wake was observed at `2026-10-12T00:35:09.844100Z`, but no durable run-specific item-by-item receipt was located in the inspected output surfaces. This stays **BEHAVIORAL UPTAKE / QUEUE-DRAIN RECEIPT NOT VERIFIED**, not “Librarian did nothing” and not “OVERDRIVE workflow is down.”

### Directive 23 accountability
- **Expected outcome:** state-machine idle, workflow liveness and Librarian directive uptake are measured separately.

- **Required action:** inspect the current scheduled Actions log/receipt when authorized; do not use a stale state timestamp as the sole failure signal. Independently produce/read back the Librarian queue-drain receipt and item-level deltas.
- **Acceptance:** recent workflow-run/log evidence or confirmed unchanged-state idle output; separately, run-specific Librarian source SHAs, pass-by-pass deltas and durable readback.
- **Dependency:** Actions run-log collection not exposed by the current connected inspection surface; no Librarian-specific drain receipt found in available output records.
